# Design Document

## Overview

This design enhances the existing job alert notification system to provide more robust, intelligent, and user-friendly periodic job notifications. The system builds upon the current architecture while adding improved frequency control, enhanced matching algorithms, user interaction tracking, and comprehensive notification management.

The enhanced system maintains the existing event-driven architecture using Kafka for job events, RabbitMQ for notification processing, and MongoDB for data persistence, while adding new components for better user experience and system reliability.

## Requirements Alignment

This design addresses the following key requirements from the requirements document:

### Requirement 1: Periodic Job Notifications
- **Keyword & Filter Storage**: Enhanced JobAlertSubscription model stores all preference types including keywords, location filters, salary range, job type, work type, experience level, and category preferences
- **Frequency Support**: System supports daily and weekly frequencies as specified in the requirements
- **Notification Methods**: Supports email, in-app notifications, and both delivery methods through the notificationMethod field
- **Job Matching**: Enhanced matching worker processes new jobs against active subscriptions using comprehensive keyword and filter matching
- **Consolidation**: Single digest approach prevents spam by grouping multiple job matches per user into consolidated notifications

### Requirement 3: Subscription Management
- **CRUD Operations**: Complete subscription management through enhanced JobAlertService with create, update, delete, and view operations
- **Status Management**: Active/inactive status management with data preservation for deactivated subscriptions
- **Subscription Limits**: Validation system prevents users from exceeding subscription limits
- **User Interface**: Clear display of all subscription details through getMyJobAlerts method

### Requirement 4: Email Formatting & Delivery
- **Rich Content**: NotificationTemplate service generates emails with job title, company name, location, salary range, and application deadline
- **Digest Format**: Multiple jobs grouped in single email with clear sections through template system
- **Action Links**: Unsubscribe and preference management links included via generateUnsubscribeLink and generatePreferenceLink methods
- **Click Tracking**: Job clicks tracked with redirect parameters through trackEmailClick method
- **Retry Logic**: 3-attempt retry mechanism with exponential backoff for failed email deliveries

### Requirement 5: System Monitoring & Analytics
- **Performance Metrics**: Comprehensive logging and analytics through Analytics service with notification metrics and system health monitoring
- **Delivery Tracking**: NotificationHistory model tracks delivery rates, open rates, and click-through rates
- **Error Handling**: Detailed error logging with administrator alerts through monitoring system
- **Queue Monitoring**: Backlog detection and status reporting for notification queues

### Requirement 6: Learning & Optimization System
- **Interaction Tracking**: JobInteraction model captures all user behaviors including views, clicks, applications, saves, and ignores
- **Adaptive Matching**: Historical interaction data influences future job prioritization through interactionScore field
- **Engagement Analysis**: Low engagement detection triggers optimization suggestions through optimizeSubscription method
- **Pattern Recognition**: System learns from user patterns to improve relevance of future job matches

## Design Rationales

### Key Design Decisions

#### Event-Driven Architecture
**Decision**: Maintain Kafka for job events and RabbitMQ for notification processing
**Rationale**: Leverages existing infrastructure while providing scalability and reliability for high-volume job processing. Kafka ensures job events are not lost, while RabbitMQ provides reliable notification delivery with retry mechanisms.

#### Dual Notification Paths
**Decision**: Separate immediate and periodic notification processing
**Rationale**: Allows urgent job matches to be delivered immediately while batching regular notifications to prevent spam. This improves user experience by ensuring important opportunities are not delayed.

#### Interaction-Based Learning
**Decision**: Track user interactions to improve future matching
**Rationale**: Addresses Requirement 6 by learning from user behavior patterns. The interactionScore field and JobInteraction model enable the system to adapt to user preferences over time, improving notification relevance.

#### Consolidated Email Digests
**Decision**: Group multiple job matches into single digest emails
**Rationale**: Prevents notification spam while ensuring users receive all relevant opportunities. This approach balances comprehensive job coverage with user experience, as specified in Requirement 1.

#### Template-Based Email System
**Decision**: Centralized NotificationTemplate service for email generation
**Rationale**: Ensures consistent email formatting across all notification types while making it easy to update templates. Supports Requirement 4 by providing rich content formatting and tracking capabilities.

#### Comprehensive Analytics
**Decision**: Separate Analytics service with detailed metrics tracking
**Rationale**: Addresses Requirement 5 by providing system administrators with detailed insights into notification performance, delivery rates, and user engagement patterns.

## Architecture

### Current System Components (Enhanced)
- **JobAlertSubscription Model**: Enhanced with new fields for notification preferences and interaction tracking
- **Matching Worker**: Improved with better matching algorithms and immediate notification logic
- **Notification Worker**: Enhanced to handle multiple notification types and delivery methods
- **Cron Jobs**: Multiple cron jobs for different notification frequencies
- **PendingNotification Model**: Enhanced with priority levels and notification metadata

### New System Components
- **NotificationPreference Model**: User-specific notification timing and delivery preferences
- **NotificationHistory Model**: Tracking of sent notifications and user interactions
- **JobInteraction Model**: Tracking user interactions with job notifications for learning
- **NotificationTemplate Service**: Centralized email template management
- **Analytics Service**: Notification performance and engagement analytics

### Enhanced Data Flow

The system processes job notifications through two main paths: immediate notifications for urgent matches and periodic digest notifications for regular updates.

```mermaid
graph TD
    A[Job Created Event] --> B[Kafka: job-events Topic]
    B --> C[Enhanced Matching Worker]
    C --> D{Match Type}
    D -->|Immediate| E[RabbitMQ: Immediate Queue]
    D -->|Periodic| F[PendingNotification Collection]
    
    G[Cron Jobs] --> H{Frequency Check}
    H -->|Daily| I[Daily Cron - 8:00 AM]
    H -->|Weekly| J[Weekly Cron - Monday 8:00 AM]
    
    I --> L[Process Daily Subscriptions]
    J --> M[Process Weekly Subscriptions]
    
    L --> O[RabbitMQ: Digest Queue]
    M --> O
    
    E --> P[Enhanced Notification Worker]
    O --> P
    
    P --> Q{Notification Method}
    Q -->|Email| R[Email Service]
    Q -->|In-App| S[In-App Notification]
    Q -->|Both| T[Both Services]
    
    R --> U[Track Delivery]
    S --> U
    T --> U
    U --> V[NotificationHistory]
```

## Components and Interfaces

### Enhanced Models

### Enhanced Workers

#### Enhanced Matching Worker
- **Immediate Matching**: Identifies high-priority jobs for immediate notifications
- **Smart Filtering**: Uses interaction history to improve job relevance
- **Duplicate Prevention**: Prevents sending the same job multiple times
- **Priority Scoring**: Assigns priority scores to job matches

#### Enhanced Notification Worker
- **Template Processing**: Uses NotificationTemplate service for consistent formatting
- **Delivery Tracking**: Tracks email delivery status and user interactions
- **Retry Logic**: Implements exponential backoff for failed deliveries
- **Rate Limiting**: Prevents spam by enforcing daily notification limits

### Enhanced Cron Jobs

#### Multiple Frequency Crons
```javascript
// Daily notifications - 8:00 AM
cron.schedule('0 8 * * *', processDailyNotifications)

// Weekly notifications - Monday 8:00 AM  
cron.schedule('0 8 * * 1', processWeeklyNotifications)

// Cleanup old data - Daily at 2:00 AM
cron.schedule('0 2 * * *', cleanupOldData)
```

# Requirements Document

## Introduction

This feature enhances the existing job alert system to provide more robust periodic job notifications based on keywords that candidates have registered for. The system will improve upon the current implementation by adding better frequency control, enhanced matching algorithms, notification preferences, and comprehensive analytics.

## Requirements

### Requirement 1

**User Story:** As a candidate, I want to receive periodic job notifications based on my registered keywords and preferences, so that I can stay updated on relevant job opportunities without manually searching.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. WHEN a candidate creates a job alert subscription THEN the system SHALL store their keyword preferences, location filters, salary range, job type, work type, experience level, and category preferences
2. WHEN a candidate specifies notification frequency THEN the system SHALL support daily, weekly.
3. WHEN a candidate chooses notification method THEN the system SHALL support email, in-app notifications, or both
4. WHEN new jobs are posted THEN the system SHALL match them against active subscriptions using keyword matching and filter criteria
5. IF a job matches multiple subscriptions for the same user THEN the system SHALL consolidate them into a single notification to avoid spam

### Requirement 3

**User Story:** As a candidate, I want to manage my job alert subscriptions easily, so that I can add, modify, active, or remove alerts as my job search needs change.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. WHEN a candidate views their subscriptions THEN the system SHALL display all active and inactive subscriptions with their details
2. WHEN a candidate updates a subscription THEN the system SHALL validate the changes and update matching criteria
3. WHEN a candidate deactivates a subscription THEN the system SHALL stop sending notifications but preserve the subscription data
4. WHEN a candidate deletes a subscription THEN the system SHALL remove all associated data and stop future notifications
5. WHEN a candidate reaches the subscription limit THEN the system SHALL prevent creation of new subscriptions and display an appropriate message

### Requirement 4

**User Story:** As a candidate, I want to receive well-formatted notification emails with relevant job details, so that I can quickly assess opportunities without visiting the platform.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. WHEN the system sends an email notification THEN it SHALL include job title, company name, location, salary range, and application deadline
2. WHEN multiple jobs match a subscription THEN the system SHALL group them in a single digest email with clear sections
3. WHEN a notification email is sent THEN it SHALL include unsubscribe and preference management links
4. WHEN a candidate clicks on a job in the email THEN it SHALL redirect to the job details page with tracking parameters
5. IF email delivery fails THEN the system SHALL retry up to 3 times and log the failure

### Requirement 5

**User Story:** As a system administrator, I want to monitor the job notification system performance, so that I can ensure reliable delivery and optimize matching algorithms.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. WHEN the system processes job matching THEN it SHALL log matching statistics and performance metrics
2. WHEN notifications are sent THEN the system SHALL track delivery rates, open rates, and click-through rates
3. WHEN errors occur in the notification pipeline THEN the system SHALL log detailed error information and alert administrators
4. WHEN the system processes periodic notifications THEN it SHALL provide metrics on processing time and notification volume
5. IF the notification queue becomes backlogged THEN the system SHALL alert administrators and provide queue status information

### Requirement 6

**User Story:** As a candidate, I want the system to learn from my interactions with job notifications, so that future recommendations become more relevant to my interests.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. WHEN a candidate clicks on jobs in notifications THEN the system SHALL track interaction patterns
2. WHEN a candidate consistently ignores certain types of jobs THEN the system SHALL gradually reduce their priority in future notifications
3. WHEN a candidate applies to jobs from notifications THEN the system SHALL boost similar job types in future matches
4. WHEN generating job matches THEN the system SHALL consider historical interaction data alongside keyword matching
5. WHEN a candidate has low engagement with notifications THEN the system SHALL suggest subscription optimization

# 02 - Quy tắc về API và Database

### 📈 Cấu trúc API Response (Bắt buộc)
Mục tiêu: Cấu trúc response phải phẳng (flat), rõ ràng và nhất quán.
Response không phân trang (lấy 1 record):
{
  "success": true,
  "message": "Lấy thông tin công việc thành công.",
  "data": { ... } // data là một object, không có meta
}

Với các request lấy danh sách có phân trang, cấu trúc response cuối cùng phải là:

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "message": "...",
  "meta": { ... },
  "data": [ ... ]
}```
Response khi có lỗi (Error Response): Đã do middleware xử lý.

**Phân chia trách nhiệm**:
- **Service** trả về: `{ meta: {...}, data: [...] }`.
- **Controller** nhận kết quả này và "trải phẳng" nó ra response cuối cùng.

### 🗃️ Database & Models (Mongoose)
- **Schema**: Luôn thêm `{ timestamps: true }`.
- **Indexes**: **BẮT BUỘC** thêm chỉ mục cho các trường thường xuyên được truy vấn.
- **Truy vấn**:
    - Luôn ưu tiên dùng `.lean()` cho các thao tác chỉ đọc.
    - Khi dùng `.populate()`, phải dùng `select` để chỉ lấy những trường cần thiết.
    - Dùng đúng `mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId` khi làm việc với ID.

# 05 - Quy trình Triển khai Tính năng (5 Bước)

Hãy triển khai các tính năng theo đúng trình tự 5 bước sau đây.

### Bước 0: Định nghĩa/Cập nhật Model & Schema
- Model (Mongoose): Nếu tính năng yêu cầu bảng mới hoặc trường mới, hãy định nghĩa nó trong src/models/.
- Schema (Zod): Định nghĩa các schema validation cho body, params, query trong một file riêng (e.g., src/schemas/job.schema.js).

### Bước 1: Định nghĩa Routes (`src/routes/....route.js`)
- Tạo endpoint, gắn middleware (authenticate, `[role]Only`, validate...).
- Ánh xạ route tới một hàm controller (dù chưa được viết).

### Bước 2: Tạo khung Controller (`src/controllers/....controller.js`)
- Tạo các hàm controller rỗng để file route không bị lỗi tham chiếu.

### Bước 3: Viết Logic trong Service (`src/services/....service.js`)
- Đây là bước cốt lõi: triển khai toàn bộ business logic.
- Tương tác với Models, kiểm tra quyền, `throw` lỗi nếu cần.
- Trả về dữ liệu đã xử lý cho controller.

### Bước 4: Hoàn thiện Controller (`src/controllers/....controller.js`)
- Lấy `userId` từ `req.user._id`.
- Gọi hàm service tương ứng.
- Định dạng kết quả trả về thành cấu trúc JSON phẳng.

# Product Overview

CareerConnect is a comprehensive job portal platform that connects job seekers (Candidates) and employers (Recruiters). The platform facilitates the entire recruitment lifecycle from job posting to hiring.

## Core Features

- **User Management**: Multi-role system supporting candidates, recruiters, and admins
- **Job Management**: Job posting, searching, and application tracking
- **Application System**: Complete application workflow with status tracking
- **Communication**: Real-time chat between candidates and recruiters
- **Interview Management**: Scheduling and management of interview processes
- **Payment System**: VNPay integration for premium features and coin-based transactions
- **AI Integration**: AI-powered features for job matching and recommendations
- **Notification System**: Real-time notifications via WebSocket and email
- **Analytics**: Comprehensive analytics and reporting for recruiters and admins

## User Roles

- **Candidate**: Job seekers who can browse jobs, apply, and manage their profiles
- **Recruiter**: Employers who post jobs, review applications, and manage hiring
- **Admin**: Platform administrators with full system access

## Key Business Logic

- Email verification required for account activation
- Coin-based economy for premium features
- Real-time messaging and notifications
- Advanced job search with hybrid search capabilities
- Interview scheduling and management system

# Project Structure

## Source Code Structure (`src/`)

### Core Application Files
- `app.js` - Express application setup and middleware configuration
- `server.js` - HTTP server initialization and startup logic

### Layered Architecture

```
src/
â”œâ”€â”€ config/                # Configuration files (DB, Redis, Kafka, etc.)
â”œâ”€â”€ constants/             # Application constants and enums
â”œâ”€â”€ controllers/           # Request handlers (thin layer)
â”œâ”€â”€ services/              # Business logic layer
â”œâ”€â”€ models/                # Mongoose schemas and database models
â”œâ”€â”€ schemas/               # Zod validation schemas
â”œâ”€â”€ routes/                # Express route definitions
â”œâ”€â”€ middleware/            # Custom middleware functions
â”œâ”€â”€ utils/                 # Utility functions and helpers
â”œâ”€â”€ queues/                # Message queue setup (RabbitMQ)
â”œâ”€â”€ socket/                # WebSocket/Socket.IO handlers
â”œâ”€â”€ cron/                  # Scheduled job definitions
â”œâ”€â”€ views/                 # Pug templates for email/HTML responses
â””â”€â”€ data/                  # Static data files and fixtures
```

## Architectural Patterns

### MVC + Services Pattern
- **Routes** â†’ **Controllers** â†’ **Services** â†’ **Models**
- Controllers are thin, delegating business logic to services
- Services contain core business logic and data manipulation
- Models define data structure and database interactions

### Validation Layer
- **Zod schemas** in `schemas/` for request validation
- Validation middleware applies schemas to routes
- Consistent error handling across all endpoints

### Background Processing
- **Workers** directory contains separate processes
- **Queues** directory manages RabbitMQ/Kafka integration
- **Cron** jobs for scheduled tasks

## File Naming Conventions

- Controllers: `*.controller.js`
- Services: `*.service.js`
- Models: PascalCase (e.g., `User.js`, `JobAlert.js`)
- Routes: `*.route.js`
- Schemas: `*.schema.js`
- Middleware: `*.middleware.js`
- Utilities: `*.js` in utils directory

# Technology Stack

## Core Technologies

- **Runtime**: Node.js 18+ with ES Modules
- **Framework**: Express.js 4.18+
- **Database**: MongoDB with Mongoose ODM
- **Authentication**: Passport.js with JWT and Google OAuth
- **Real-time**: Socket.IO for WebSocket connections
- **Caching**: Redis for session management and caching
- **Message Queue**: RabbitMQ and Kafka for async processing
- **File Storage**: Cloudinary for image/document uploads
- **Email**: Nodemailer with SMTP
- **Payment**: Zalopay integration
- **Validation**: Zod for schema validation

## Key Libraries

- **Security**: Helmet, CORS, bcryptjs, express-rate-limit
- **Utilities**: Moment.js, UUID, crypto-js, chalk, winston (logging)
- **Template Engine**: Pug for email templates
- **Development**: Nodemon, Babel, npm-run-all

## Common Commands

### Development
```bash
# Start development server with hot reload
npm run dev

# Start all services (main app + workers)
npm run start:all

# Start individual workers
npm run worker:notification
npm run worker:matching
```


## Architecture Notes

- ES Modules throughout (type: "module" in package.json)
- Async/await pattern with express-async-handler
- Microservice-ready with worker processes
- Event-driven architecture using message queues
